- **proposal-to-pr**: Drive an OpenSpec change from proposal to merge-ready PR. Reads `openspec/changes/&lt;name&gt;/`, blocks at Stage 1 until every prerequisite named in the proposal's `&gt; **Depends on:**` blockquote has reached `main`, plants a canary and runs parallel reviews on the proposal, refines until zero blockers, implements each task, verifies with an auto-fix loop that knows the ugrep baseline, re-reviews the diff, and opens a PR seeded with review evidence. Never merges. Resumable — state persists in `.git/nullius/pipeline/&lt;name&gt;.state.json`.
